PASS __tests__/unit/green-mask-gate.test.js
  green-mask classifier — mixesSuccessAndError
    √ flags an array mixing a 2xx with a 4xx/5xx (the green-mask) (2 ms)
    √ catches hex-encoded status codes too (0xC8=200, 0x190=400) (1 ms)
    √ does NOT flag a pure-error array (legitimate negative assertion) (1 ms)
    √ does NOT flag a pure-success array (1 ms)
    √ ignores non-status numbers (1 ms)
  green-mask scanner — a focused test can never be waived
    √ reports .only even when the line carries a green-mask-allow comment (2 ms)
    √ reports the fit / fdescribe aliases too (2 ms)
  green-mask scanner — scope cannot silently narrow
    √ covers apps/backend/__tests__ ROOT, not only its integration/ + unit/ subdirs (1 ms)
    √ covers co-located component tests under apps/web-app/src (1 ms)
    √ scans the whole corpus, not a subset (floor well above the old 558) (1 ms)
    √ skips only build/vendor directories (1 ms)
  green-mask classifier — QA #808 evasions stay closed
    √ catches hex-only status arrays (0xC8 = 200, 0x194 = 404) (3 ms)
    √ catches a hex success mixed with a decimal error (16 ms)
    √ does not flag a pure-error array (false-positive guard) (3 ms)
    √ honours green-mask-allow on a tolerant array, but never on focus (4 ms)
    √ DOES NOT catch the two documented limits — multi-line and var-extract (35 ms)

    √ (ค) state ถูกแต่ส่งซ้ำรอบสอง → รอบแรกผ่าน รอบสองถูกปฏิเสธ (single-use) (52 ms)
    √ ไม่มี cookie เลย (ส่งแต่ body.state) → 400 AUTH_STATE_INVALID (20 ms)

    √ happy → 200: mint ผ่าน issueTokensForAuthenticatedUser + provider_token httpOnly cookie (57 ms)

    √ ทะเบียนปฏิเสธ fail-closed (auto-provision ปิด) → catalog status + ไม่มี create/upsert/mint + audit FAILURE (21 ms)
    √ บัญชีที่ผูกเลข 13 หลักเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ → 403 ไม่ auto-link ไม่ create/upsert/mint + audit FAILURE (26 ms)
